Kept\n * as a shared function rather than copied into the repair path because a second\n * renderer that drifted would make a repaired home quietly differ from a freshly\n * launched one — the exact drift class the codex-gateway-config extraction was\n * done to end (WI-3645).\n *\n * The per-session client identity is baked into the url because codex cannot\n * env-expand config.toml, and the superuser bearer rides as an http_headers\n * Authorization (D-006 — the env-var form doesn't deliver on codex 0.135).\n * `env_http_headers` supplies a legacy process-environment native-session claim.\n * The MCP host prefers Codex's per-call _meta.threadId, since the process can\n * inherit a parent's CODEX_SESSION_ID while shell tools receive the current id.\n * Both carriers are checked against the owner's registered native incarnation.\n */\nexport function suCodexConfigToml(opts: SuCodexConfigInput): string {\n  const url = mcpUrlWithClient(opts.mcpUrl, opts.sid);\n  const gatewayConfig = codexGatewayConfigToml(opts.codexGatewayAccountId, {\n    gatewayOn: opts.codexGatewayAuto,\n    ownerId: opts.sid,\n    priority: opts.codexGatewayPriority,\n  });\n  // Keep the effective model in the per-session root config so a resume that\n  // has no CLI `-m` flag cannot fall through to CODEX_HOME/models_cache.json.\n  const resolvedModel = resolveCodexModel(opts.model);\n  const modelConfig = codexModelConfigToml(resolvedModel);\n  const contextConfig = codexContextConfigToml(resolvedModel, {\n    codexHome: opts.codexHome ?? undefined,\n    compactionLimit: opts.compactionLimit,\n  }).trimEnd();\n  return [\n    ...(contextConfig ? contextConfig.split('\\n') : []),\n    ...modelConfig,\n    ...(opts.sqliteHome ? [`sqlite_home = \"${tomlEscape(opts.sqliteHome)}\"`] : []),\n    ...(opts.logDir ? [`log_dir = \"${tomlEscape(opts.logDir)}\"`] : []),\n    ...gatewayConfig.root,\n    ...CODEX_DANGER_TOML,\n    codexIsolationPreamble(opts.headless),\n    ...inheritedCodexTrustToml(opts.trustDir, opts.denyAmbientContext !== true),\n    ...gatewayConfig.tables,\n    ...CODEX_TUI_TOML,\n    '# Per-session codex SU home config (managed by bootstrap-su / writeSuCodexHome).',\n    '[mcp_servers.papercusp-su]',\n    `url = \"${tomlEscape(url)}\"`,\n    // Papercusp tools can declare a 600s deadline plus a 5s MCP buffer.\n    // Keep Codex's per-server client timeout just above that contract.\n    'tool_timeout_sec = 610',\n    ...(opts.token ? [`http_headers = { Authorization = \"Bearer ${tomlEscape(opts.token)}\" }`] : []),\n    'env_http_headers = { \"x-papercusp-native-session\" = \"CODEX_SESSION_ID\" }',\n    ...projectHttpMcpToml(opts.denyAmbientContext ? null : opts.projectDir ?? opts.trustDir),\n    '',\n  ].join('\\n');\n}\n\n/**\n * REPAIR an existing CODEX_HOME's `config.toml` in place — the resume-leg\n * counterpart to `writeSuCodexHome`, and deliberately NOT that function.\n *\n * `writeSuCodexHome` rebuilds generated launch files while retaining native\n * transcripts. A resume must also retain its prompt, auth and SQLite state;\n * it must not run the fresh-launch reset. This repairs\n * only managed launch files in place: config.toml plus identity-bearing hooks /\n * diagnostics when their owner has drifted. Rollouts, prompts and auth survive.\n *\n * WI-38706: the archiver had been unlinking these configs (it took the home's\n * shared state under one thread's session id), leaving codex to re-create an\n * 87-byte trust-only stub on the next launch — no MCP server, no approvals\n * bypass, no gateway provider — so the resume either died on the missing provider\n * or came up silently uncoordinated. `existsSync(home)` is required, not created:\n * a home that is not there has no rollouts to resume and must not be conjured.\n *\n * Idempotent: a home already carrying `[mcp_servers.papercusp-su]` AND the\n * context roots required by its recorded launch model is left byte-identical\n * and reported `repaired: false`. Older managed homes that predate the roots\n * (or carry superseded values) are rewritten from this canonical renderer so a\n * model-less resume cannot silently fall back to Codex's smaller native window.\n */\nfunction ensureSuCodexHomeConfigImpl(opts: SuCodexConfigInput & {\n  sessionKey: string | number;\n  /** Canonical, current SU render; supplied by the operator repair route. */\n  promptText?: string;\n  /** A carry starts a fresh thread, so a GC-removed home can be recreated safely. */\n  recoverMissingHome?: boolean;\n}, ownerSidIo?: CodexHomeOwnerSidIo): {\n  codexHome: string;\n  sqliteHome: string;\n  configPath: string;\n  repaired: boolean;\n  identityRepaired: boolean;\n  promptRepaired: boolean;\n  reason?: 'no-home';\n} {\n  const codexHome = codexHomeForSessionKey(opts.sessionKey);\n  const sqliteHome = preservedCodexSqliteHome(codexHome, opts.sessionKey);\n  const configPath = join(codexHome, 'config.toml');\n  if (!existsSync(codexHome)) {\n    if (!opts.recoverMissingHome || !opts.promptText?.trim()) {\n      return { codexHome, sqliteHome, configPath, repaired: false, identityRepaired: false, promptRepaired: false, reason: 'no-home' };\n    }\n    mkdirSync(codexHome, { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });\n    if (!opts.denyAmbientContext) inheritUserCodexHome(codexHome);\n    ensureCodexSkillWatchRoots({ codexHome });\n  }\n  // The recorded home can be repaired without the original launch options.\n  // Reuse its existing diagnostics rather than granting ambient inheritance.\n  const diagnosticsPath = join(codexHome, 'papercusp-diagnostics.json');\n  let diagnostics: Record<string, unknown> = {};\n  try {\n    const parsed = JSON.parse(readFileSync(diagnosticsPath, 'utf8'));\n    if (parsed && typeof parsed === 'object' && !Array.isArray(parsed)) diagnostics = parsed;\n    if (diagnostics.denyAmbientContext === true) {\n      opts = { ...opts, denyAmbientContext: true };\n    }\n  } catch { /* Legacy/unconfined homes have no saved context policy. */ }\n  const contextPolicyRepaired = opts.denyAmbientContext === true && diagnostics.denyAmbientContext !== true;\n  if (contextPolicyRepaired) {\n    writeFileSync(diagnosticsPath, `${JSON.stringify({ ...diagnostics, denyAmbientContext: true }, null, 2)}\\n`, { mode: 0o600 });\n  }\n  mkdirSync(sqliteHome, { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });\n  const logDir = join(codexHome, 'log');\n  mkdirSync(logDir, { recursive: true });\n  const resolvedModel = resolveCodexModel(opts.model);\n  const expectedModelConfig = codexModelConfigToml(resolvedModel).join('\\n');\n  const expectedContextConfig = codexContextConfigToml(resolvedModel, {\n    codexHome,\n    compactionLimit: opts.compactionLimit,\n  });\n  const expectedLogDir = `log_dir = \"${tomlEscape(logDir)}\"`;\n  const expectedSqliteHome = `sqlite_home = \"${tomlEscape(sqliteHome)}\"`;\n  const expectedClient = `client=${encodeURIComponent(opts.sid)}`;\n  const expectedNativeSessionHeader = 'env_http_headers = { \"x-papercusp-native-session\" = \"CODEX_SESSION_ID\" }';\n  const expectedSuMcpRoute =\n    '[mcp_servers.papercusp-su]\\nurl = \"' +\n    tomlEscape(mcpUrlWithClient(opts.mcpUrl, opts.sid)) +\n    '\"\\ntool_timeout_sec = 610';\n  const expectedGateway = codexGatewayConfigToml(opts.codexGatewayAccountId, {\n    gatewayOn: opts.codexGatewayAuto,\n    ownerId: opts.sid,\n    priority: opts.codexGatewayPriority,\n  });\n  const expectedProjectMcp = projectHttpMcpToml(opts.denyAmbientContext ? null : opts.projectDir ?? opts.trustDir).join('\\n');\n  let configReady = false;\n  try {\n    const current = readFileSync(configPath, 'utf8');\n    const modelMatches = current.includes(expectedModelConfig);\n    const contextMatches = modelMatches && (expectedContextConfig\n      ? current.includes(expectedContextConfig.trimEnd())\n      : !/^model_(?:context_window|auto_compact_token_limit)\\s*=/m.test(current));\n    const gatewayReady = expectedGateway.root.length\n      ? current.includes(expectedGateway.root.join('\\n')) && current.includes(expectedGateway.tables.join('\\n'))\n      : !current.includes('PAPERCUSP_CODEX_GATEWAY_ROOT') && !current.includes('PAPERCUSP_CODEX_GATEWAY_PROVIDER');\n    const hasHeadlessPluginsPolicy = current.includes('plugins = false');\n    const hasHeadlessAuthPolicy = current.includes('auth_elicitation = false');\n    const headlessPolicyReady = opts.headless === true\n      ? hasHeadlessPluginsPolicy && hasHeadlessAuthPolicy\n      : !hasHeadlessPluginsPolicy && !hasHeadlessAuthPolicy;\n    configReady =\n      current.includes(expectedSuMcpRoute) &&\n      current.includes(expectedProjectMcp) &&\n      current.includes(expectedClient) &&\n      current.includes(expectedNativeSessionHeader) &&\n      parseToml(current).check_for_update_on_startup === false &&\n      contextMatches &&\n      current.includes(expectedSqliteHome) &&\n      current.includes(expectedLogDir) &&\n      current.includes('[tui]\\nterminal_title = []\\nalternate_screen = \"never\"\\n') &&\n      gatewayReady &&\n      headlessPolicyReady &&\n      (!opts.denyAmbientContext || current === suCodexConfigToml({ ...opts, logDir, codexHome, sqliteHome }));\n  } catch {\n    /* absent or unreadable — that is precisely the repair case */\n  }\n  if (!configReady) {\n    writeFileSync(configPath, suCodexConfigToml({ ...opts, logDir, codexHome, sqliteHome }), { mode: 0o600 });\n  }\n  const identity = repairSuCodexHomeIdentityImpl(\n    codexHome,\n    opts.sid,\n    opts.sessionKey,\n    mcpUrlWithClient(opts.mcpUrl, opts.sid),\n    ownerSidIo,\n  );\n  const agentsPath = join(codexHome, 'AGENTS.md');\n  const carryBasePath = join(codexHome, '.papercusp-carry-base-AGENTS.md');\n  const readBase = (path: string): string => {\n    try {\n      return readFileSync(path, 'utf8').split(/\\n---\\n## Managed carry checkpoint\\b/)[0].trim();\n    } catch {\n      return '';\n    }\n  };\n  // The carry minter prefers its base snapshot over AGENTS.md. Repair BOTH\n  // whenever either is empty, otherwise a prior empty snapshot wins again.\n  const agentsBase = readBase(agentsPath);\n  const snapshotExists = existsSync(carryBasePath);\n  const snapshotBase = snapshotExists ? readBase(carryBasePath) : agentsBase;\n  const promptRepaired = !!opts.promptText?.trim() && (!agentsBase || !snapshotBase);\n  if (promptRepaired) {\n    const lockRuntime = readCodexLockRuntimeVerdict({\n      ownerId: opts.sid,\n      hooksConfigured: identity.hooksInstalled,\n    });\n    const canonicalPrompt = codexAgentsPrompt(opts.promptText!, lockRuntime);\n    writeFileSync(agentsPath, canonicalPrompt, { mode: 0o600 });\n    writeFileSync(carryBasePath, canonicalPrompt, { mode: 0o600 });\n  }\n  return {\n    codexHome,\n    sqliteHome,\n    configPath,\n    repaired: !configReady || identity.repaired || promptRepaired || contextPolicyRepaired,\n    identityRepaired: identity.repaired,\n    promptRepaired,\n  };\n}\n\ntype EnsureSuCodexHomeConfigOptions = SuCodexConfigInput & {\n  sessionKey: string | number;\n  /** Canonical, current SU render; supplied by the operator repair route. */\n  promptText?: string;\n  /** A carry starts a fresh thread, so a GC-removed home can be recreated safely. */\n  recoverMissingHome?: boolean;\n};\n\nexport function ensureSuCodexHomeConfig(opts: EnsureSuCodexHomeConfigOptions) {\n  return ensureSuCodexHomeConfigImpl(opts);\n}\n\nexport async function ensureSuCodexHomeConfigAsync(\n  opts: EnsureSuCodexHomeConfigOptions,\n): Promise<ReturnType<typeof ensureSuCodexHomeConfigImpl>> {\n  const codexHome = codexHomeForSessionKey(opts.sessionKey);\n  return withCodexHomeAsyncWriteLock(codexHome, async () => {\n    const priorSid = await existingCodexHomeOwnerSidAsync(codexHome);\n    const result = await runCodexHomeMaterializerJob<ReturnType<typeof ensureSuCodexHomeConfigImpl>>({\n      operation: 'su-ensure-config', options: opts, priorSid, homeDir: homedir(),\n    });\n    if (result.identityRepaired) await stampCodexHomeOwnerSidAsync(codexHome, opts.sid);\n    return result;\n  });\n}\n\ntype WriteSuCodexHomeOptions = {\n  /** Same host-owned context ceiling consulted by bootstrap and MCP. */\n  denyAmbientContext?: true;\n  sessionKey: string | number;\n  /** Superuser MCP url (?superuser=1&workspace=…) from buildLaunchSpec(su). */\n  mcpUrl: string;\n  /** The engineer playbook — becomes AGENTS.md. */\n  promptText: string;\n  /** Per-session coordination identity, baked into the url's &client=. */\n  sid: string;\n  /** Launch model. Extended-window models bake their canonical root keys into\n   *  config.toml so model-less resumes retain the launch window. */\n  model?: string | null;\n  /** Per-session soft compaction ceiling recovered from the recorded psu argv. */\n  compactionLimit?: number | null;\n  /** Superuser bearer token, baked as an http_headers Authorization (D-006).\n   *  Omit only when no token is installed → a degraded non-superuser home. */\n  token?: string | null;\n  /** Optional Codex account pin; writes a custom model provider that points Codex at the inference gateway. */\n  codexGatewayAccountId?: string | null;\n  /** Route through the gateway with NO pin (explicit-auto, WI-3645): writes the SAME provider\n   *  block with the x-papercusp-account header omitted — the gateway auto-selects. Ignored\n   *  when codexGatewayAccountId is set. */\n  codexGatewayAuto?: boolean;\n  /** Gateway admission label; `su` for interactive SU homes. */\n  codexGatewayPriority?: string | null;\n  /** Suppress optional interactive plugin authentication in an unattended TUI. */\n  headless?: boolean;\n  /** The directory the operator is launching this SU session into (resolveSuLaunchCwd).\n   *  Seeded as `[projects.\"<dir>\"] trust_level = \"trusted\"` so a fresh CODEX_HOME\n   *  does not block at codex's \"Do you trust this directory?\" boot prompt. */\n  trustDir?: string | null;\n  /** Canonical launch-time mode/route/scope state for the machine-readable\n   * instruction precedence trace carried by the exact AGENTS.md lint. */\n  instructionRuntime?: InstructionRuntimeContext;\n  /** EI-11366 identity-split guard: liveness check for a PRIOR occupant of this\n   *  sessionKey's home directory. Injected (not statically imported) so this\n   *  module stays dependency-free/pure for its unit tests — the real production\n   *  check is `findLiveHost` (psu-pty-discovery), wired by bootstrap-su.ts.\n   *  Omitted ⇒ the collision check is skipped entirely (byte-identical to the\n   *  pre-guard behavior — safe default for every existing caller/test). */\n  isOwnerLive?: (sid: string) => boolean;\n};\n\nfunction writeSuCodexHomeImpl(opts: WriteSuCodexHomeOptions, ownerSidIo?: CodexHomeOwnerSidIo): SuCodexHome {\n  const targetHome = codexHomeForSessionKey(opts.sessionKey);\n  // EI-11366: a live Codex psu host was once found split across two identities\n  // — the physical host process/PAPERCUSP_SID/socket stayed with its ORIGINAL\n  // sid while CODEX_HOME/hooks.json on disk had been silently regenerated for a\n  // DIFFERENT sid. `freshCodexHome` below rebuilds generated launch files but\n  // retains native transcripts, including on re-preparing the SAME logical\n  // session. The identity guard still checks whether the home being rebuilt is\n  // still the ACTIVE home of a DIFFERENT, currently-live session. If a future\n  // caller (a retry path, a sessionKey computed by different code, an operator\n  // debug script) ever passes a sessionKey that collides with a still-live\n  // OTHER sid's home, this converts a silent identity-split into a loud,\n  // diagnosable refusal instead. Fails OPEN (proceeds, current behavior) unless\n  // BOTH a differing prior owner AND a caller-supplied liveness check confirm\n  // it is still live — never blocks a normal fresh/re-prepare launch.\n  const priorSid = ownerSidIo ? ownerSidIo.priorSid : existingCodexHomeOwnerSid(targetHome);\n  if (priorSid && priorSid !== opts.sid && opts.isOwnerLive?.(priorSid)) {\n    throw new Error(\n      `writeSuCodexHome: refusing to rebuild ${targetHome} — it is the LIVE Codex home of a ` +\n        `different session (owner ${priorSid}); overwriting it would silently split that ` +\n        `session's identity between its still-running process and a rewritten CODEX_HOME ` +\n        `(EI-11366). Pass the SAME sid to re-prepare that session, or a sessionKey that does ` +\n        `not collide with a live session's home.`,\n    );\n  }\n  const codexHome = freshCodexHome(targetHome, opts.denyAmbientContext !== true);\n  const sqliteHome = freshCodexSqliteHome(opts.sessionKey);\n  const inheritedPromptsCopied = !opts.denyAmbientContext && copyInheritedCodexPrompts(codexHome);\n\n  const agentsPath = join(codexHome, 'AGENTS.md');\n\n  // Bake the per-session client identity into the url (codex can't env-expand\n  // config.toml) + the superuser bearer as an http_headers Authorization\n  // (D-006 — the env-var form doesn't deliver on codex 0.135).\n  const configPath = join(codexHome, 'config.toml');\n  const logDir = join(codexHome, 'log');\n  mkdirSync(logDir, { recursive: true });\n  writeFileSync(configPath, suCodexConfigToml({ ...opts, logDir, codexHome: targetHome, sqliteHome }), { mode: 0o600 });\n\n  // SU sessions pass coordSid so the merged activity hook's coord fold stays\n  // ON (new coord:inbox messages folded into the SAME activity:report round\n  // trip, parity with the Claude global-settings coord entry, EI-11405) — an\n  // SU/engineer session is a worker too, so it gets the activity bridge either\n  // way; both keyed to the session id.\n  const hooksInstalled = writeCodexLockHooks(codexHome, {\n    lockSid: opts.sid,\n    coordSid: opts.sid,\n    activitySid: opts.sid,\n    advSessionId: opts.sessionKey,\n    objectiveSid: opts.sid,\n    injectSid: opts.sid,\n    mcpUrl: mcpUrlWithClient(opts.mcpUrl, opts.sid),\n    mcpUsesSuperuserToken: true,\n  });\n  const lockRuntime = readCodexLockRuntimeVerdict({ ownerId: opts.sid, hooksConfigured: hooksInstalled });\n  const agentsText = codexAgentsPrompt(opts.promptText, lockRuntime);\n  const instructionLint = lintInstructionText(agentsText, 20, opts.instructionRuntime);\n  writeFileSync(agentsPath, agentsText, { mode: 0o600 });\n  writeCodexHomeDiagnostics(codexHome, {\n    hooksInstalled,\n    inheritedPromptsCopied,\n    denyAmbientContext: opts.denyAmbientContext === true,\n    lockOwnerSid: opts.sid,\n    lockRuntime,\n  });\n  // EI-11366: stamp the sentinel the collision guard above reads on the NEXT\n  // write to this sessionKey's home. Best-effort — a write failure here must\n  // never fail the launch (mirrors every other diagnostics write in this fn).\n  if (!ownerSidIo?.skipStamp) {\n    try {\n      writeFileSync(join(codexHome, '.owner-sid'), opts.sid, { mode: 0o600 });\n    } catch {\n      /* non-fatal: the collision guard simply skips on the next write */\n    }\n  }\n\n  return { codexHome, sqliteHome, agentsPath, configPath, instructionLint };\n}\n\nexport function writeSuCodexHome(opts: WriteSuCodexHomeOptions): SuCodexHome {\n  return writeSuCodexHomeImpl(opts);\n}\n\nexport async function writeSuCodexHomeAsync(\n  opts: Omit<WriteSuCodexHomeOptions, 'isOwnerLive'> & {\n    isOwnerLive?: (sid: string) => boolean | Promise<boolean>;\n  },\n): Promise<SuCodexHome> {\n  const targetHome = codexHomeForSessionKey(opts.sessionKey);\n  return withCodexHomeAsyncWriteLock(targetHome, async () => {\n    const priorSid = await existingCodexHomeOwnerSidAsync(targetHome);\n    if (priorSid && priorSid !== opts.sid && opts.isOwnerLive && await opts.isOwnerLive(priorSid)) {\n      throw new Error(\n        `writeSuCodexHome: refusing to rebuild ${targetHome} — it is the LIVE Codex home of a ` +\n          `different session (owner ${priorSid}); overwriting it would silently split that ` +\n          `session's identity between its still-running process and a rewritten CODEX_HOME ` +\n          `(EI-11366). Pass the SAME sid to re-prepare that session, or a sessionKey that does ` +\n          `not collide with a live session's home.`,\n      );\n    }\n    const { isOwnerLive: _isOwnerLive, ...writeOpts } = opts;\n    const result = await runCodexHomeMaterializerJob<SuCodexHome>({\n      operation: 'su-write', options: writeOpts, priorSid, homeDir: homedir(),\n    });\n    await stampCodexHomeOwnerSidAsync(result.codexHome, opts.sid);\n    return result;\n  });\n}\n\n/** Worker entry dispatch. Keep the synchronous implementations here so their\n *  existing filesystem and identity semantics remain the single source of truth. */\nexport function executeCodexHomeMaterializerJob(job: CodexHomeMaterializerJob): unknown {\n  const previousHome = materializerHomeOverride;\n  materializerHomeOverride = job.homeDir ?? previousHome;\n  try {\n    switch (job.operation) {\n      case 'role-write':\n        return writeRoleCodexHome(job.options as WriteRoleCodexHomeOptions);\n      case 'su-write':\n        return writeSuCodexHomeImpl(job.options as WriteSuCodexHomeOptions, {\n          priorSid: job.priorSid ?? null,\n          skipStamp: true,\n        });\n      case 'su-ensure-config':\n        return ensureSuCodexHomeConfigImpl(job.options as EnsureSuCodexHomeConfigOptions, {\n          priorSid: job.priorSid ?? null,\n          skipStamp: true,\n        });\n    }\n  } finally {\n    materializerHomeOverride = previousHome;\n  }\n}\n\nconst PAPERCUSP_SU_TABLE_RE = /^mcp_servers\\.(?:papercusp-su|\"papercusp-su\")(?:\\.|$)/;\n\n/**\n * The SU `config.toml` minus what makes it an SU session: the papercusp-su MCP\n * table (and any of its sub-tables) and the psu cap on the project's AGENTS.md.\n * Every other key and table, the project's own MCP servers included, is kept.\n */\nexport function codingAssistantCodexConfigToml(toml: string): string {\n  const out: string[] = [];\n  let skipping = false;\n  for (const line of toml.split('\\n')) {\n    const header = /^\\s*\\[\\[?([^\\]]+)\\]\\]?\\s*(?:#.*)?$/.exec(line);\n    if (header) skipping = PAPERCUSP_SU_TABLE_RE.test(header[1].replace(/\\s+/g, ''));\n    if (skipping) continue;\n    if (/^\\s*project_doc_max_bytes\\s*=\\s*0\\s*(?:#.*)?$/.test(line)) continue;\n    if (line.startsWith('# psu prompt isolation')) continue;\n    out.push(line);\n  }\n  return out.join('\\n');\n}\n\n/**\n * pui-chat-first-ux-2026-09-28 P-016 (D-004/D-005 for Codex): a PUI chat opened\n * outside every registered checkout runs Codex's own identity. This strips the\n * SU pieces from its already-written per-session home IN PLACE, so rollouts,\n * auth and the project's own MCP servers survive: the papercusp-su MCP server,\n * the cap that stops Codex reading the project's AGENTS.md, the SU playbook\n * (replaced by a COPY of the user's own `~/.codex/AGENTS.md`, never a symlink,\n * because a later SU repair writes through that path), and the managed\n * hooks.json. Idempotent. Run it on every engine start: an exact resume first\n * repairs the home back to its SU configuration.\n */\nexport function makeCodingAssistantCodexHome(codexHome: string, home: string = homedir()): void {\n  const configPath = join(codexHome, 'config.toml');\n  if (existsSync(configPath)) {\n    const current = readFileSync(configPath, 'utf8');\n    const next = codingAssistantCodexConfigToml(current);\n    if (next !== current) writeFileSync(configPath, next, { mode: 0o600 });\n  }\n  const agentsPath = join(codexHome, 'AGENTS.md');\n  rmSync(agentsPath, { force: true });\n  const userAgents = join(home, '.codex', 'AGENTS.md');\n  if (existsSync(userAgents)) {\n    // Older standalone installers put the SU playbook in the user's global\n    // instructions. It is generated SU context, not a personal instruction:\n    // copying it back would undo the identity isolation above (EI-25332697642301259).\n    // Use the installer's exact paired markers; leave unmarked user rules alone.\n    const personal = readFileSync(userAgents, 'utf8').replace(\n      /<!-- BEGIN papercusp-su playbook \\(managed by install-standalone-mcp\\.sh\\) -->[\\s\\S]*?<!-- END papercusp-su playbook -->\\r?\\n?/g,\n      '',\n    );\n    if (personal.trim()) writeFileSync(agentsPath, personal, { mode: 0o600 });\n  }\n  rmSync(join(codexHome, 'hooks.json'), { force: true });\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "packages/operator-core/lib/role-codex-home.test.ts",
      "workingSha256": "bc5dd0d6097bc6d951bfe30bb51997bbb127f958b2527c5bac1f73205a6326ee",
      "committedSha256": "99bb16de72b9bc1735bd0bf560ceb6eb0140764995eeacecb89e395e33a87f4b",
      "exactMatch": false,
      "workingContent": "/**\n * Unit tests for writeRoleCodexHome — the per-session CODEX_HOME builder\n * for interactive codex role sessions (agent-launch-unification).\n *\n * Asserts the on-disk layout codex needs: AGENTS.md = role prompt,\n * config.toml = [mcp_servers.papercusp] url = \"<signed>\", auth.json\n * symlinked to the real ~/.codex/auth.json (never copied).\n */\nimport { spawn, spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';\nimport {\n  chmodSync,\n  copyFileSync,\n  existsSync,\n  lstatSync,\n  mkdirSync,\n  mkdtempSync,\n  readFileSync,\n  readlinkSync,\n  rmSync,\n  symlinkSync,\n  writeFileSync,\n} from 'node:fs';\nimport { createServer } from 'node:http';\nimport { tmpdir } from 'node:os';\nimport { join } from 'node:path';\nimport { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';\nimport { parse as parseToml } from '@iarna/toml';\nimport { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';\n\n// os.homedir() reads the OS password DB on Linux, NOT $HOME — so we mock it\n// to a throwaway dir. (A holder var, set per-test, keeps the hoisted mock in\n// sync.) tmpdir stays real.\nconst homeHolder = { home: '' };\nvi.mock('node:os', async (importActual) => {\n  const actual = await importActual<typeof import('node:os')>();\n  return { ...actual, homedir: () => homeHolder.home };\n});\nconst ownerSidSyncCalls = vi.hoisted(() => [] as string[]);\nvi.mock('node:fs', async (importActual) => {\n  const actual = await importActual<typeof import('node:fs')>();\n  const isOwnerSid = (path: unknown) => String(path).endsWith('.owner-sid');\n  return {\n    ...actual,\n    existsSync: (...args: Parameters<typeof actual.existsSync>) => {\n      if (isOwnerSid(args[0])) ownerSidSyncCalls.push('existsSync');\n      return actual.existsSync(...args);\n    },\n    readFileSync: (...args: Parameters<typeof actual.readFileSync>) => {\n      if (isOwnerSid(args[0])) ownerSidSyncCalls.push('readFileSync');\n      return actual.readFileSync(...args);\n    },\n    writeFileSync: (...args: Parameters<typeof actual.writeFileSync>) => {\n      if (isOwnerSid(args[0])) ownerSidSyncCalls.push('writeFileSync');\n      return actual.writeFileSync(...args);\n    },\n  };\n});\n\nlet fakeHome = '';\nconst RESOLVED_CODEX_DEFAULT = 'gpt-5.6-sol:xhigh';\nconst savedCodexSqliteHomes = process.env.PAPERCUSP_CODEX_SQLITE_HOMES_DIR;\nconst savedSuCodexHomes = process.env.PAPERCUSP_SU_CODEX_HOMES_DIR;\n\nbeforeEach(() => {\n  ownerSidSyncCalls.length = 0;\n  fakeHome = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'role-codex-home-test-'));\n  homeHolder.home = fakeHome;\n  process.env.PAPERCUSP_SU_CODEX_HOMES_DIR = join(fakeHome, '.papercusp', 'su-codex-homes');\n  process.env.PAPERCUSP_CODEX_SQLITE_HOMES_DIR = join(fakeHome, 'codex-sqlite-homes');\n  vi.resetModules();\n});\n\nafterEach(() => {\n  if (fakeHome) rmSync(fakeHome, { recursive: true, force: true });\n  if (savedCodexSqliteHomes === undefined) delete process.env.PAPERCUSP_CODEX_SQLITE_HOMES_DIR;\n  else process.env.PAPERCUSP_CODEX_SQLITE_HOMES_DIR = savedCodexSqliteHomes;\n  if (savedSuCodexHomes === undefined) delete process.env.PAPERCUSP_SU_CODEX_HOMES_DIR;\n  else process.env.PAPERCUSP_SU_CODEX_HOMES_DIR = savedSuCodexHomes;\n});\n\nfunction withResolvedDefault<T extends (opts: any) => any>(fn: T): T {\n  return ((opts: Parameters<T>[0]) =>\n    fn({ model: RESOLVED_CODEX_DEFAULT, ...opts })) as T;\n}\n\nasync function load() {\n  return withResolvedDefault(\n    (await import('./role-codex-home')).writeRoleCodexHome,\n  );\n}\n\nasync function loadMod() {\n  const mod = await import('./role-codex-home');\n  return {\n    ...mod,\n    writeRoleCodexHome: withResolvedDefault(mod.writeRoleCodexHome),\n    writeSuCodexHome: withResolvedDefault(mod.writeSuCodexHome),\n    writeSuCodexHomeAsync: withResolvedDefault(mod.writeSuCodexHomeAsync),\n    ensureSuCodexHomeConfig: withResolvedDefault(mod.ensureSuCodexHomeConfig),\n    ensureSuCodexHomeConfigAsync: withResolvedDefault(mod.ensureSuCodexHomeConfigAsync),\n  };\n}\n\n/** Install the exact stale cache shape that caused D-007 to rewrite the\n * documented 1M/900k opt-in as 872k/784.8k on real hosts. */\nfunction installStaleExtendedWindowRecord(model: string) {\n  const dir = join(fakeHome, '.codex');\n  mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });\n  writeFileSync(\n    join(dir, 'models_cache.json'),\n    JSON.stringify({\n      models: [\n        {\n          slug: model,\n          context_window: 272_000,\n          max_context_window: 872_000,\n          effective_context_window_percent: 95,\n        },\n      ],\n    }),\n  );\n}\n\n/** Drop stub CC lock-hook scripts under the fake home so writeCodexLockHooks\n *  has something to point at (it no-ops when they're absent). */\nasync function installFakeHooks(options: { turnJournal?: boolean } = {}) {\n  const { mkdirSync } = await import('node:fs');\n  const dir = join(fakeHome, '.papercusp', 'hooks', 'cc');\n  mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });\n  writeFileSync(join(dir, 'pretooluse-locks-acquire.sh'), '#!/bin/sh\\n');\n  writeFileSync(join(dir, 'posttooluse-locks-release.sh'), '#!/bin/sh\\n');\n  writeFileSync(join(dir, 'posttooluse-activity-report.sh'), '#!/bin/sh\\n');\n  writeFileSync(join(dir, 'lifecycle-report.sh'), '#!/bin/sh\\n');\n  if (options.turnJournal) writeFileSync(join(dir, 'stop-turn-journal.sh'), '#!/usr/bin/env bash\\n');\n  writeFileSync(join(dir, 'precompact-managed-carry.mjs'), '#!/usr/bin/env node\\n');\n  writeFileSync(join(dir, 'posttooluse-objective-title.sh'), '#!/bin/sh\\n');\n  writeFileSync(join(dir, 'posttooluse-write-byte-integrity-guard.mjs'), '#!/usr/bin/env node\\n');\n  writeFileSync(join(dir, 'pretooluse-control-bytes-content-guard.mjs'), '#!/usr/bin/env node\\n');\n  writeFileSync(join(dir, 'pretooluse-generated-file-edit-guard.mjs'), '#!/usr/bin/env node\\n');\n  writeFileSync(join(dir, 'pretooluse-unreserved-migration-guard.mjs'), '#!/usr/bin/env node\\n');\n  writeFileSync(join(dir, 'pretooluse-bash-resource-gate.sh'), '#!/bin/sh\\n');\n  writeFileSync(join(dir, 'userpromptsubmit-provenance.sh'), '#!/usr/bin/env bash\\n');\n  return dir;\n}\n\nfunction installOwnerRuntimeProof(owner: string) {\n  const dir = join(fakeHome, '.papercusp', 'locks-cache');\n  const safe = owner.replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9._-]/g, '_');\n  mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });\n  writeFileSync(\n    join(dir, `owner-${safe}-last-decision.json`),\n    JSON.stringify({ ts: new Date().toISOString(), owner, decision: 'granted' }),\n  );\n  writeFileSync(join(dir, `owner-${safe}-last-success.json`), JSON.stringify({ ts: new Date().toISOString(), owner }));\n}\n\ndescribe('fresh Codex home transcript custody', () => {\n  it.each(['writeRoleCodexHome', 'writeSuCodexHome', 'writeSuCodexHomeAsync'] as const)(\n    '%s retains original native transcripts while rebuilding generated launch files',\n    async (writerName) => {\n      const mod = await loadMod();\n      const writer = mod[writerName];\n      const opts = { sessionKey: 'native-custody', sid: 'su-native-custody', mcpUrl: 'http://x/?sig=old', promptText: 'OLD PROMPT' };\n      const first = await writer(opts);\n      const bytes = Buffer.from('{\"type\":\"response_item\",\"text\":\"original receipt — $literal\"}\\n');\n      const transcripts = [\n        join(first.codexHome, 'sessions', '2026', '10', '09', 'rollout-2026-10-09T00-00-00-01a12212-c9fe-7921-bc00-6644cf505bab.jsonl'),\n        join(first.codexHome, 'archived_sessions', 'rollout-earlier.jsonl'),\n      ];\n      for (const transcript of transcripts) {\n        mkdirSync(join(transcript, '..'), { recursive: true });\n        writeFileSync(transcript, bytes);\n      }\n      writeFileSync(join(first.codexHome, 'STALE.txt'), 'disposable launch state');\n      const second = await writer({ ...opts, promptText: 'CURRENT PROMPT', mcpUrl: 'http://x/?sig=current' });\n      for (const transcript of transcripts) expect(readFileSync(transcript)).toEqual(bytes);\n      expect(second.codexHome).toBe(first.codexHome);\n      expect(readFileSync(second.agentsPath, 'utf8')).toContain('CURRENT PROMPT');\n      expect(readFileSync(second.configPath, 'utf8')).toContain('sig=current');\n      expect(existsSync(join(second.codexHome, 'STALE.txt'))).toBe(false);\n      // Keeping the original rollout also keeps the existing archive guard\n      // effective: a later retention pass cannot mistake this for an empty home.\n      const { findUnarchivedSessionDirs, planSessionDirGc } = await import('./session-dir-gc');\n      const candidates = [{ path: second.codexHome, keyKind: 'session' as const, key: 'native-custody', mtimeMs: 0 }];\n      const readStamp = vi.fn(async () => null);\n      const unarchivedPaths = await findUnarchivedSessionDirs(candidates, { store: { readStamp } });\n      expect(readStamp).toHaveBeenCalledWith('codex', '01a12212-c9fe-7921-bc00-6644cf505bab');\n      const plan = planSessionDirGc({ candidates, protectedIdentity: { ownerIds: new Set(), sessionKeys: new Set() },\n        nowMs: 2 * 86400_000, retentionMs: 86400_000, unarchivedPaths });\n      expect(plan.remove).toEqual([]);\n      expect(plan.keptUnarchived).toEqual(candidates);\n    },\n  );\n\n  it.each(['sessions', 'archived_sessions'])(\n    'refuses a symlinked %s transcript root before deleting generated launch files',\n    async (name) => {\n      const writer = await load();\n      const opts = { sessionKey: 'linked-custody', mcpUrl: 'http://x/?sig=original', promptText: 'ORIGINAL PROMPT' };\n      const first = writer(opts);\n      const elsewhere = join(fakeHome, 'other-native-history');\n      mkdirSync(elsewhere);\n      const transcript = join(elsewhere, 'original.jsonl');\n      writeFileSync(transcript, 'original receipt');\n      symlinkSync(elsewhere, join(first.codexHome, name), 'dir');\n      expect(() => writer({ ...opts, promptText: 'REPLACEMENT' })).toThrow(/transcript root.*real directory/);\n      expect(readFileSync(first.agentsPath, 'utf8')).toContain('ORIGINAL PROMPT');\n      expect(lstatSync(join(first.codexHome, name)).isSymbolicLink()).toBe(true);\n      expect(readFileSync(transcript, 'utf8')).toBe('original receipt');\n    },\n  );\n});\n\ndescribe('writeRoleCodexHome', () => {\n  it('repairs a role home in place while preserving its exact native transcript and SQLite data', async () => {\n    const writer = await load();\n    const input = { sessionKey: 4242, mcpUrl: join(first.codexHome, 'sessions', 'native.jsonl');\n    const database = join(first.sqliteHome, 'state.sqlite');\n    writeFileSync(transcript, 'native history');\n    writeFileSync(database, 'native state');\n    const replacementRoot = join(fakeHome, 'different-serving-tmp-root');\n    process.env.PAPERCUSP_CODEX_SQLITE_HOMES_DIR = replacementRoot;\n    const repaired = writer({ ...input, expect(existsSync(replacementRoot)).toBe(false);\n    expect(readFileSync(repaired.agentsPath, 'utf8')).toContain('CURRENT RESEARCH ROLE');\n    expect(readFileSync(repaired.configPath, path and refuses a relative recorded path', async () => {\n    process.env.PAPERCUSP_CODEX_SQLITE_HOMES_DIR = join(fakeHome, 'state with \"quotes\"');\n    const writer = await load();\n    const expect(isOwnerLive).toHaveBeenCalledWith('su-first');\n    expect(await readFile(ownerSidPath, 'utf8')).toBe('su-first');\n    expect(ownerSidSyncCalls).toEqual([]);\n  });\n\n  it('uses async import('node:fs/promises');\n    const { writeSuCodexHome } = await loadMod();\n    const opts = {\n      sessionKey: 'async-owner-sid-repair',\n      mcpUrl: 'http://x/api/mcp?superuser=1',\n repaired: false, identityRepaired: false });\n\n    await writeFile(ownerSidPath, 'su-stale');\n    const repaired = await ensureSuCodexHomeConfigAsync(opts);\n    expect(repaired).toMatchObject({ repaired: true, identityRepaired: true });\n    expect(await readFile(ownerSidPath, 'utf8')).toBe('su-repair');\n    expect(ownerSidSyncCalls).toEqual([]);\n\n    // WI-6554700010009552: that repair ran in a worker thread, where the node:os\n    // mock does not apply. Its hook paths must come from the CALLER's home (the\n    // fake home), never the worker's own os.homedir() (the real host home).\n    const hooks = JSON.parse(await readFile(join(home.codexHome, 'hooks.json'), 'utf8')) as {\n      hooks: Record<string, Array<{ hooks?: Array<{ command?: string }> }>>;\n    };\n    const scriptCommands = Object.values(hooks.hooks)\n      .flat()\n      .flatMap((group) => (group.hooks ?? ''))\n      .filter((command) => command.includes('/.papercusp/hooks/'));\n    expect(scriptCommands.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n    expect(scriptCommands.filter((command) => !command.includes(fakeHome))).toEqual([]);\n  });\n\n  it('keeps production SU creation and repair paths on the async owner-sid APIs', () => {\n    const bootstrap = readFileSync(new URL('./endpoint-route/routes/agent-mcp/bootstrap-su.ts', import.meta.url), 'utf8');\n    const wakeExecutor = readFileSync(new URL('./events/await/wake-executor.ts', import.meta.url), 'utf8');\n    const advSessions = readFileSync(new URL('./endpoint-route/routes/adv/sessions.ts', import.meta.url), 'utf8');\n\n    expect(bootstrap).toMatch(/await writeSuCodexHomeAsync\\s*\\(/);\n    expect(wakeExecutor).toMatch(/await ensureSuCodexHomeConfigAsync\\s*\\(/);\n    expect(advSessions).toMatch(/ensureConfig\\s*=\\s*\\([^)]*\\)\\s*=>\\s*ensureSuCodexHomeConfigAsync\\s*\\(/);\n    expect(advSessions.match(/await ensureConfig\\s*\\(/g) ?? []).toHaveLength(2);\n  });\n});\n\n/**\n * CONTEXT-INJECTION registration — codex-context-injection-parity-2026-08-09 P-004.\n *\n * These assert the codex leg of the shared dispatcher (D-001) is wired into the\n * per-session CODEX_HOME. The premise they rest on was MEASURED live, not assumed:\n * codex-cli 0.146.0 DOES fire a `UserPromptSubmit` MatcherGroup declared in\n * `$CODEX_HOME/hooks.json` (D-004) — the file's own prior comment claimed no such\n * event existed there, which is why this is asserted rather than trusted.\n */\ndescribe('writeCodexLockHooks — context-injection hooks (P-004)', () => {\n  /** Stub the shared dispatcher at its installed path (installInjectionHooks\n   *  puts it at ~/.papercusp/hooks/inject/index.mjs, BESIDE the cc hooks). */\n  function installFakeDispatcher() {\n    const dir = join(fakeHome, '.papercusp', 'hooks', 'inject');\n    mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });\n    writeFileSync(join(dir, 'index.mjs'), '#!/usr/bin/env node\\n');\n  }\n\n  function injectEntries(hooks: any, event: string) {\n    return (hooks.hooks[event] ?? []).filter((e: { hooks: { command: string }[] }) =>\n      e.hooks[0].command.includes('hooks/inject/index.mjs'),\n    );\n  }\n\n  function provenanceEntries(hooks: any) {\n    return (hooks.hooks.UserPromptSubmit ?? []).filter((e: { hooks: { command: string }[] }) =>\n      e.hooks.some((hook) => hook.command.includes('userpromptsubmit-provenance.sh')),\n    );\n  }\n\n  it('registers BOTH ports through the ONE shared dispatcher, with the right --client/--event', async () => {\n    await installFakeHooks();\n    installFakeDispatcher();\n    const writeRoleCodexHome = await load();\n    const out = writeRoleCodexHome({\n      sessionKey: 40,\n      mcpUrl: 'http://x/?role=worker&sig=1',\n      promptText: 'P',\n      sid: 'role-inject-40',\n    });\n    const hooks = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(out.codexHome, 'hooks.json'), 'utf8'));\n\n    // turn-start ← UserPromptSubmit (the port carrying ~94% of recall volume)\n    const turnStart = injectEntries(hooks, 'UserPromptSubmit');\n    expect(turnStart).toHaveLength(1);\n    expect(turnStart[0].hooks[0].command).toContain('--client=codex');\n    expect(turnStart[0].hooks[0].command).toContain('--event=UserPromptSubmit');\n\n    const provenance = provenanceEntries(hooks);\n    expect(provenance).toHaveLength(1);\n    expect(provenance[0].matcher).toBe('.*');\n    expect(provenance[0].hooks[0].command).toContain(\"PAPERCUSP_SID='role-inject-40'\");\n    expect(provenance[0].hooks[0].command).toContain('PAPERCUSP_AGENT=codex');\n\n    // mid-turn ← PostToolUse. PER CALL on codex (it has no batch event, D-002 §5).\n    const midTurn = injectEntries(hooks, 'PostToolUse');\n    expect(midTurn).toHaveLength(1);\n    expect(midTurn[0].hooks[0].command).toContain('--event=PostToolUse');\n\n    // The dispatcher is SHARED — a codex-specific transport copy would be the\n    // D-001 violation this whole seam exists to prevent.\n    expect(JSON.stringify(hooks)).not.toContain('adapters/codex.mjs');\n  });\n\n  it('BAKES PAPERCUSP_SID — an un-baked sid makes the hook fire and inject NOTHING', async () => {\n    // This is the falsifiability control for D-001 invariant 3: codex hands hook\n    // commands a minimal env, and the dispatcher treats a missing PAPERCUSP_SID as\n    // \"not a psu session\" → exit 0, no output. That failure is INVISIBLE (a hook\n    // that ran, succeeded, and injected nothing looks identical to one that never\n    // fired), so the bake is asserted explicitly rather than left to review.\n    await installFakeHooks();\n    installFakeDispatcher();\n    const writeRoleCodexHome = await load();\n    const out = writeRoleCodexHome({\n      sessionKey: 41,\n      mcpUrl: 'http://x/?role=worker&sig=1',\n      promptText: 'P',\n      sid: 'role-inject-41',\n    });\n    const hooks = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(out.codexHome, 'hooks.json'), 'utf8'));\n    for (const event of ['UserPromptSubmit', 'PostToolUse']) {\n      const [entry] = injectEntries(hooks, event);\n      expect(entry, `${event} injection hook missing`).toBeTruthy();\n      expect(entry.hooks[0].command).toContain(\"PAPERCUSP_SID='role-inject-41'\");\n      expect(entry.hooks[0].command).toContain('PAPERCUSP_AGENT=codex');\n    }\n  });\n\n  it('omits the dispatcher when it is absent while keeping installed owner-provenance capture', async () => {\n    await installFakeHooks(); // provenance hook present; dispatcher deliberately absent\n    const writeRoleCodexHome = await load();\n    const out = writeRoleCodexHome({\n      sessionKey: 42,\n      mcpUrl: 'http://x/?role=worker&sig=1',\n      promptText: 'P',\n      sid: 'role-inject-42',\n    });\n    const hooks = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(out.codexHome, 'hooks.json'), 'utf8'));\n    // The dispatcher is omitted rather than pointing to a missing path, while\n    // the separately installed owner-provenance hook remains registered.\n    expect(injectEntries(hooks, 'PostToolUse')).toHaveLength(0);\n    expect(injectEntries(hooks, 'UserPromptSubmit')).toHaveLength(0);\n    expect(provenanceEntries(hooks)).toHaveLength(1);\n    // ...and the pre-existing lock/activity hooks are UNAFFECTED.\n    expect(JSON.stringify(hooks)).toContain('pretooluse-locks-acquire.sh');\n  });\n\n  it('registers NOTHING when the session has no sid', async () => {\n    await installFakeHooks();\n    installFakeDispatcher();\n    const writeRoleCodexHome = await load();\n    const out = writeRoleCodexHome({ sessionKey: 43, mcpUrl: 'http://x/?role=worker&sig=1', promptText: 'P' });\n    const hooks = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(out.codexHome, 'hooks.json'), 'utf8'));\n    expect(hooks.hooks.UserPromptSubmit).toBeUndefined();\n    expect(injectEntries(hooks, 'PostToolUse')).toHaveLength(0);\n  });\n\n  it('SU homes get injection too (su sessions are the dominant codex surface)', async () => {\n    await installFakeHooks();\n    installFakeDispatcher();\n    const { writeSuCodexHome } = await loadMod();\n    const out = writeSuCodexHome({\n      sessionKey: 44,\n expect(injectEntries(hooks, 'UserPromptSubmit')).toHaveLength(1);\n    expect(injectEntries(hooks, 'PostToolUse')).toHaveLength(1);\n    expect(injectEntries(hooks, 'UserPromptSubmit')[0].hooks[0].command).toContain(\"PAPERCUSP_SID='su-inject-44'\");\n  });\n\n  it('registers the three identity hook ports (P-011) through the same dispatcher, every matcher .*', async () => {\n    await installFakeHooks();\n    installFakeDispatcher();\n    const writeRoleCodexHome = await load();\n    const out = writeRoleCodexHome({ sessionKey: 45, mcpUrl: 'http://x/?role=worker&sig=1', promptText: 'P', sid: 'role-inject-45' });\n    const hooks = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(out.codexHome, 'hooks.json'), 'utf8'));\n    for (const event of ['PreToolUse', 'Stop', 'SessionStart']) {\n      const entries = injectEntries(hooks, event);\n      expect(entries, `${event} identity hook`).toHaveLength(1);\n      expect(entries[0].matcher).toBe('.*');\n      expect(entries[0].hooks[0].command).toContain(`--client=codex --event=${event}`);\n      expect(entries[0].hooks[0].command).toContain(\"PAPERCUSP_SID='role-inject-45'\");\n    }\n    // The lifecycle bridge keeps its own SessionStart entry beside the identity one.\n    expect(JSON.stringify(hooks.hooks.SessionStart)).toContain('lifecycle-report.sh');\n  });\n\n  it('keeps lifecycle Stop cleanup but omits identity ports without the dispatcher', async () => {\n    await installFakeHooks();\n    const writeRoleCodexHome = await load();\n    const out = writeRoleCodexHome({ sessionKey: 46, mcpUrl: 'http://x/?role=worker&sig=1', promptText: 'P', sid: 'role-inject-46' });\n    const hooks = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(out.codexHome, 'hooks.json'), 'utf8'));\n    expect(hooks.hooks.Stop.some((group: any) =>\n      group.hooks.some((hook: any) => hook.command.includes('lifecycle-report.sh')),\n    )).toBe(true);\n    expect(injectEntries(hooks, 'PreToolUse')).toHaveLength(0);\n    expect(injectEntries(hooks, 'Stop')).toHaveLength(0);\n    expect(injectEntries(hooks, 'SessionStart')).toHaveLength(0);\n  });\n});\n\n/**\n * WI-10006795 — the codex turn-end JOURNAL hook.\n *\n * Measured before the fix: journal:record-turn had 1 codex call in 7 days against\n * ~13k Claude calls, because only Claude's settings.json enrolled\n * stop-turn-journal.sh. A codex GOAL holder therefore produced no\n * goalPlacementTurnEnd rows. These pin the enrollment, the resume self-repair of a\n * pre-fix home, and (end to end) that the composed command really reaches the\n * operator as a codex turn under the minimal env codex hands its hooks.\n */\ndescribe('writeCodexLockHooks — turn-end journal (WI-10006795)', () => {\n  const REAL_CC_HOOKS = fileURLToPath(new URL('../../../apps/operator/scripts/hooks/cc/', import.meta.url));\n\n  function journalEntries(hooks: any) {\n    return (hooks.hooks.Stop ?? []).filter((e: { hooks: { command: string }[] }) =>\n      e.hooks[0].command.includes('stop-turn-journal.sh'),\n    );\n  }\n\n  /** The real script plus the two python helpers it imports, so the e2e case runs\n   *  the shipped hook rather than a stub. Returns the fake cc hooks dir. */\n  async function installRealJournalHook() {\n    const dir = await installFakeHooks();\n    for (const name of ['stop-turn-journal.sh', 'pc_nested_cli.py', 'mcp_response.py']) {\n      copyFileSync(join(REAL_CC_HOOKS, name), join(dir, name));\n      chmodSync(join(dir, name), 0o755);\n    }\n    return dir;\n  }\n\n  it('enrolls the journal on the Stop event of SU and role homes, sid and agent baked', async () => {\n    await installRealJournalHook();\n    const { writeSuCodexHome, writeRoleCodexHome } = await loadMod();\n    const su = writeSuCodexHome({ sessionKey: 61, mcpUrl: 'http://x/api/mcp?superuser=1', promptText: 'P', sid: 'su-journal-61' });\n    const role = writeRoleCodexHome({ sessionKey: 62, mcpUrl: 'http://x/?role=worker&sig=1', promptText: 'P', sid: 'role-journal-62' });\n    for (const [out, sid] of [[su, 'su-journal-61'], [role, 'role-journal-62']] as const) {\n      const hooks = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(out.codexHome, 'hooks.json'), 'utf8'));\n      const entries = journalEntries(hooks);\n      expect(entries, `${sid} journal entry`).toHaveLength(1);\n      expect(entries[0].matcher).toBe('.*');\n      expect(entries[0].hooks[0].command).toContain(`PAPERCUSP_SID='${sid}'`);\n      expect(entries[0].hooks[0].command).toContain('PAPERCUSP_AGENT=codex');\n    }\n  });\n\n  it('calibration: no journal entry when the script is not installed', async () => {\n    await installFakeHooks();\n    const { writeSuCodexHome } = await loadMod();\n    const out = writeSuCodexHome({ sessionKey: 63, mcpUrl: 'http://x/api/mcp?superuser=1', promptText: 'P', sid: 'su-journal-63' });\n    const hooks = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(out.codexHome, 'hooks.json'), 'utf8'));\n    expect(journalEntries(hooks)).toHaveLength(0);\n  });\n\n  it('a resumed pre-fix home is repaired once, then left byte-identical', async () => {\n    await installFakeHooks();\n    const { ensureSuCodexHomeConfig, writeSuCodexHome } = await loadMod();\n    // Written while the script was absent: the shape every codex home had before the fix.\n    const home = writeSuCodexHome({\n      sessionKey: 'journal-upgrade',\n      mcpUrl: 'http://x/api/mcp?superuser=1',\n      promptText: 'PRESERVE',\n      sid: 'su-journal-upgrade',\n    });\n    const hooksPath = join(home.codexHome, 'hooks.json');\n    expect(journalEntries(JSON.parse(readFileSync(hooksPath, 'utf8')))).toHaveLength(0);\n\n    await installRealJournalHook();\n    const resume = () =>\n      ensureSuCodexHomeConfig({\n        sessionKey: 'journal-upgrade',\n        mcpUrl: 'http://x/api/mcp?superuser=1',\n        sid: 'su-journal-upgrade',\n      });\n    expect(resume()).toMatchObject({ identityRepaired: true });\n    const repairedText = readFileSync(hooksPath, 'utf8');\n    expect(journalEntries(JSON.parse(repairedText))).toHaveLength(1);\n    expect(readFileSync(join(home.codexHome, 'AGENTS.md'), 'utf8')).toContain('PRESERVE');\n\n    // No repair loop: a current home is not rewritten on the next resume.\n    expect(resume()).toMatchObject({ identityRepaired: false });\n    expect(readFileSync(hooksPath, 'utf8')).toBe(repairedText);\n  });\n\n  it.each([\n    { delivery: 'synchronous success', synchronous: true, outcome: 'success' },\n    { delivery: 'detached success', synchronous: false, outcome: 'success' },\n    { delivery: 'detached blocked response', synchronous: false, outcome: 'blocked' },\n    { delivery: 'detached HTTP failure', synchronous: false, outcome: 'failure' },\n    { delivery: 'detached connection refusal', synchronous: false, outcome: 'refused' },\n  ])('end to end: the composed Stop command preserves a codex turn with $delivery', async ({ synchronous, outcome }) => {\n    await installRealJournalHook();\n    writeFileSync(join(fakeHome, '.papercusp', 'superuser-token'), 'test-token\\n');\n    const { writeSuCodexHome } = await loadMod();\n    const out = writeSuCodexHome({ sessionKey: 64, mcpUrl: 'http://x/api/mcp?superuser=1', promptText: 'P', sid: 'su-journal-e2e' });\n    const hooks = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(out.codexHome, 'hooks.json'), 'utf8'));\n    const [entry] = journalEntries(hooks);\n    expect(entry, 'journal entry composed').toBeTruthy();\n\n    const bodies: string[] = [];\n    let releaseResponse: (() => void) | undefined;\n    let responseReleased = false;\n    const server = createServer((req, res) => {\n      let body = '';\n      req.on('data', (chunk) => (body += chunk));\n      req.on('end', () => {\n        bodies.push(body);\n        res.setHeader('content-type', 'application/json');\n        const reply = () => {\n          responseReleased = true;\n          res.statusCode = outcome === 'failure' ? 503 : 200;\n          res.end(JSON.stringify({ jsonrpc: '2.0', id: 1, result: { content: [] } }));\n        };\n        if (outcome === 'blocked') releaseResponse = reply;\n        else reply();\n      });\n    });\n    await new Promise<void>((done) => server.listen(0, '127.0.0.1', done));\n    const address = server.address();\n    if (!address || typeof address === 'string') throw new Error('fixture server did not bind');\n    if (outcome === 'refused') await new Promise<void>((done) => server.close(() => done()));\n\n    // A deterministic process shape: hook → fake `codex` → fake psu-launcher.mjs,\n    // which pc_nested_cli.py classifies as the managed incarnation whatever runs\n    // this test (CI, a psu claude, an operator-spawned runner).\n    const fixtures = join(fakeHome, 'journal-e2e');\n    mkdirSync(fixtures, { recursive: true });\n    const codexBody = join(fixtures, 'fake-codex-body.sh');\n    writeFileSync(codexBody, 'eval \"$HOOK_CMD\"\\nexit $?\\n');\n    const launcher = join(fixtures, 'psu-launcher.mjs');\n    writeFileSync(launcher, '( exec -a codex bash \"$FAKE_CODEX_BODY\" )\\nexit $?\\n');\n\n    // Codex hands hooks a minimal env: the identity must come from the COMMAND.\n    const env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = {\n      ...process.env,\n      HOME: fakeHome,\n      PAPERCUSP_OPERATOR_URL: `http://127.0.0.1:${address.port}`,\n      HOOK_CMD: entry.hooks[0].command,\n      FAKE_CODEX_BODY: codexBody,\n    };\n    delete env.PAPERCUSP_SID;\n    delete env.PAPERCUSP_AGENT;\n    if (synchronous) env.PAPERCUSP_JOURNAL_SYNC = '1';\n    else delete env.PAPERCUSP_JOURNAL_SYNC;\n    const transcript = join(fixtures, 'rollout.jsonl');\n    writeFileSync(transcript, '{}\\n');\n    // The codex-cli 0.160 `stop.command.input` shape (required fields).\n    const payload = {\n      hook_event_name: 'Stop',\n      session_id: 'codex-native-64',\n      transcript_path: transcript,\n      turn_id: 'turn-1',\n      stop_hook_active: false,\n      cwd: fixtures,\n      model: 'gpt-5.6-sol',\n      permission_mode: 'default',\n      last_assistant_message: null,\n    };\n    const child = spawn('bash', ['-c', 'exec -a node bash \"$0\"', launcher], { env, stdio: ['pipe', 'pipe', 'pipe'] });\n    let closed: { code: number | null; stdout: string; stderr: string } | undefined;\n    const completion = new Promise<NonNullable<typeof closed>>((done, fail) => {\n      let stdout = '';\n      let stderr = '';\n      child.stdout.on('data', (c) => (stdout += c));\n      child.stderr.on('data', (c) => (stderr += c));\n      child.on('error', fail);\n      child.on('close', (code) => {\n        closed = { code, stdout, stderr };\n        done(closed);\n      });\n      child.stdin.end(JSON.stringify(payload));\n    });\n    try {\n      if (outcome === 'blocked') {\n        await vi.waitFor(() => expect(bodies).toHaveLength(1), { timeout: 3_000 });\n        // The request actually reached the server, but its response stays held.\n        // A synchronous POST or an inherited output pipe cannot satisfy this.\n        await vi.waitFor(() => expect(closed).toBeDefined(), { timeout: 2_000 });\n        expect(responseReleased).toBe(false);\n        releaseResponse!();\n      }\n      const result = await completion;\n      expect(result.code).toBe(0);\n      // Codex parses Stop stdout as a decision; the journal must never emit one.\n      expect(result.stdout).toBe('');\n      expect(result.stderr).toBe('');\n      if (outcome === 'refused') {\n        expect(bodies).toHaveLength(0);\n        return;\n      }\n      // Detached success/error delivery may arrive after the hook has exited.\n      await vi.waitFor(() => expect(bodies).toHaveLength(1), { timeout: 3_000 });\n      expect(bodies).toHaveLength(1);\n      const rpc = JSON.parse(bodies[0]);\n      expect(rpc.params.name).toBe('journal:record-turn');\n      expect(rpc.params.arguments).toEqual({\n        owner: 'su-journal-e2e',\n        agent: 'codex',\n        session_id: 'codex-native-64',\n        source_kind: 'codex',\n        transcript_path: transcript,\n      });\n    } finally {\n      if (!responseReleased) releaseResponse?.();\n      if (child.exitCode === null && child.signalCode === null) child.kill();\n      await completion;\n      if (server.listening) {\n        server.closeAllConnections();\n        await new Promise<void>((done) => server.close(() => done()));\n      }\n    }\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('makeCodingAssistantCodexHome (pui-chat-first-ux P-016)', () => {\n  async function suHome() {\n    const { writeSuCodexHome } = await import('./role-codex-home');\n
